## 14:09:00 PST: C-1 root cause (developer code read, accepted) and fix plan
- C-1 cause: create-room does replace(room) and then pushes Invite as a modal. Invite has no Done/dismiss (swipe only). Later pushes then render inside that modal sheet. This affects every host after creating a room, not only deep links. The keyboard offset inside the sheet comes up short by about the sheet's top inset (hypothesis).
- Fix (a) is approved and staged on a branch with no merge: Invite gets a Done/Close button that dismisses it, and the room is never inside a modal. Fix (b) (keyboard math) only if needed. C-2: native clear() next to setDraft(''). The supervisor reviews; the merge happens only in a named gap. Re-check afterwards: on the host, dismiss Invite, then R1 → composer focus.
- No new room is used for this (1 room left, reserved for R2).

## 20:35 PST: Face-match test plan review — ACCEPT WITH CHANGES; 3 human decisions (supervisor)
Reviewed docs/face-match-connection-test-plan.md and scripts/face-match-check.mjs (read in full), plus face-match/index.ts. Server read at ~04:33Z: face_enrollments/face_jobs/face_matches 0/0/0, face-refs 0 objects. Cron jobs are only room-janitor and expiry-reminders, so nothing calls face-match automatically. R2 016c49bf (code 19455) has 1 member (host) and 1 ready photo, and expires 2026-11-08T01:52:32Z. 1 pre-existing anonymous user.
Confirmed good: auth before the switch (401 then 503). The 503 returns before any job claim, so a call with the switch off sends nothing to AWS. Jobs are scoped to the caller's own enrollments, batch 10, MAX_ATTEMPTS bound. The script prints IDs only and writes its state file outside the repo with mode 600. Only the publishable key is used. Threshold 90. R1–R7 are all covered.
Required changes:
- FM-P1: `probe` creates a new anonymous user on every run (at least 2 runs = 2 extra auth users) that are not in the R6 cleanup list. Either add every probe user ID to the cleanup and the state file, or make the post-run probe reuse A's token from the state file. The pre-run probe user must also be deleted by exact ID.
- FM-P2: shrink the switch window. Split `run` into `prepare`: users, join, A2/B1 upload, A1 ref upload, enroll_face, and a probe as A that must return 503 (this proves the jobs exist and nothing ran), all with the switch OFF. Then `match`: one face-match call as A plus the result read, the only step between T1 and T2. A setup error (media insert/upload/RLS) then fails with the switch off, and the window is a single call.
- FM-P3: after T2, also count face_matches, and record the job statuses (done/failed + attempts) before cleanup, so the call count can be checked: calls = sum of attempts on the test jobs.
Notes (not blocking):
- N-1: anonymous users cannot host (create_room rejects is_anonymous). Option (b) "new room" therefore needs a new email host account, which uses more budget. Option (a) reuse R2 needs no new room. Its extra CompareFaces call goes to the existing QA fixture photo (a no-face QA image, not a participant photo), and it gives R2's expiry deletion more data to remove. Supervisor recommendation: (a).
- N-2: after cleanup, the users' "joined" system messages stay in R2 with sender null until R2 expires (same as D1). Acceptable.
- N-3: during the window nobody opens Photos/Me on the sims. Host 17 should not open R2's gallery, so the test photos are not cached on the device outside server cleanup.
- N-4: if the call returns 503 right after T1 (secret propagation delay), wait and retry at most once and log it. A 200 probe after T2 means the switch is not off: stop and report.
Human decisions needed (routed via supervisor):
- D-a: reuse R2 (recommended) or a new room plus email host account.
- D-b: OK to create 2 temporary anonymous users (A, B; plus the probe user unless it is reused), deleted by exact ID the same night. These are auth users, so the 1-account budget does not silently cover them.
- D-c: who sets FACE_MATCH_ENABLED. Recommended: the human sets and unsets it in the dashboard, and the developer only probes. Alternative: the developer runs `supabase secrets set` with an explicit OK given for this one run.
Gate unchanged: no switch flip until FM-P1..P3 are done (I re-read the script), D-a/b/c are answered, and the human says steps 1–5 are done.
- 20:30 AWS IAM (human screenshot): user moment-face-worker, 1 active key, only policy MomentFaceCompareOnly = Allow rekognition:CompareFaces on * . Step 2 DONE (least privilege OK). Evidence aws-iam-policy-compareonly.png. Waiting: Supabase Secrets names screenshot. (qa)

## 23:50 PST: review criteria for USER_DECISIONS 23:47 (J-1 Join shift, R-1 freehand route) (supervisor)
J-1 Join screen shift (17 Pro, docs/evidence/2026-11-06-bug-join-17pro-shifted.png: label and field go under the header when focused)
- J1-a Root cause named, in mobile/src/app/join/index.tsx: which inset/keyboard/scroll behavior moves the content. No blind padding constant.
- J1-b With the field focused, the "5-DIGIT CODE" label, the field and Continue are all visible below the header on 17 Pro, 17, 17e and the real iPhone. Keyboard dismiss restores the layout.

- R1-a Host only, same as today's route editing. Guests see the route read-only and get no pen.
- R1-b While drawing: no pan, zoom, rotate or pitch, and no POI/building/label selection (the Apple place card must not open; this also closes F5-2).
  Camera frozen with heading 0 and pitch 0, or the projection handles them.
- R1-c Screen→coordinate conversion: name the API you use (check the expo-maps SDK 58 docs). If you use your own Web-Mercator projection from the frozen camera, it needs
  a unit test with a known center/zoom/size, and you state the error at the screen edges. A drawn line must land where the finger went (device check).
- R1-d Point budget: simplify (e.g. distance threshold or Douglas-Peucker) to ≤ MAX_ROUTE_POINTS (2000) before set_route. Never silently truncate:
  toRoutePayload currently does slice(0, 2000), which would cut the end of a long stroke. Unit tests for the simplification and the cap.
- R1-e Done saves through the existing set_route RPC only (no migration, no new table). Cancel or back discards the stroke, and the old route is kept.
  Empty or one-point strokes are not saved. Edit route reopens with the current route shown. Decide whether a new stroke replaces the route or continues it, and state the choice.
- R1-f After Done the map unfreezes, the camera fits the route (existing cameraFor), and guests update through the room broadcast.
- R1-g State whether the tap-to-add path is removed or kept. Keep the F5-1 "Show my location" behavior unchanged.
Both: npx tsc --noEmit, npx expo lint and npm test green; a separate branch per item; no native rebuild unless needed (say if a pod or config change forces one).

Code cause: useRoomMedia (mobile/src/lib/media.ts) marks loading when the filter key changes, but reload() only runs from useRoomEvents' onStatus/onEvent, and that subscription depends on roomId only (lib/room-channel.ts). A filter change never triggers a fetch, so settledKey never matches. "All" works because it is the initial key.
  FM-Q (quality): Photos of me misses group photos and some solo photos. Likely causes in code: Rekognition compares against the 480px-wide JPEG 0.7 preview (media.ts PREVIEW_WIDTH), so group faces are a few dozen px; QualityFilter AUTO drops small/blurry faces; match threshold 90. Not yet checked against logs (Supabase MCP keychain locked in qa).

### Migration 0027 + qrewing_ai_test.sql, developer ddfe985 (parent ed5a281): static SQL review PASS
- Matches spec 1.1 to 1.8. RLS is select-only through is_room_member, with own-row-only access for qrewing_members and qrewing_matches. There are no client write grants. The three RPCs are authenticated-only and raise 42501 or 22023. The cascades come from rooms and room_members.
- broadcast_room_change (0018) is compatible: room_id is read from the row, the id is null and gets stripped, and it fires on live rooms only.
- Rulings on the developer's 8 choices are all approved: log_qrewing_usage is service_role only; the triage cap counts message_triage rows with state <> 'skipped' in the last 24 h; consent needs ai_enabled and withdrawing also clears mute; the triage state checks; the recap trigger; entry needs membership only; the helper grants; the Edge Function filters matches to current members.
- Notes (non-blocking): (N1) the Edge Function must derive message_triage.room_id from the messages row, not from the request body. (N2) For the live rollback run with DDL pasted after BEGIN, put `set local lock_timeout = '3s'` before the DDL.
- NOT RUN: there is no local Postgres. The first execution will be the human-approved live rollback run (via qa). Nothing has been applied or deployed.

### developer-pace c7363d2, 7b4a87e, 3cd0054 (queue items 3-5): code-review PASS with one should-fix
- Supervisor re-ran in the qrewing-pace worktree at 3cd0054: npm test 349/349 pass, tsc 0. Simulator, dark-mode screenshots and spoken cues were not run.
- Checked: only kept fixes feed recent pace and the stationary check; re-anchoring happens only after 3 agreeing dropped fixes and adds no distance; a gap of 90 s or more is never read as 3 minutes still; paceRecapLine returns null with no session; getActivitySummary never rejects; the copy fix is correct (location is shared, history stays on the phone).
- P1 (should-fix, correctness): stepFix compares the distance from lastFix (the anchor, which a pause fix does not move) with dt from lastAtMs (which a pause fix does move). After pauses, slow creep or drift is therefore counted over too short a time. Supervisor repro (pace-core.ts imported in node): 0.4 m/s for 300 s in 30 s heartbeats gave distanceM 119.9 and durationS 150. That is double the real speed, and those steps should have been pauses. Fix: measure the pause and jump speed from the same reference point as the distance (move lastFix on a pause, or use lastFix.atMs). Add this repro as a test.
- Product change routed to qa (proxy): durationS, the average and the splits now use moving time (steps under 0.5 m/s add nothing). This is standard for pace stats; the supervisor sees no technical objection.

### Migration 0027 grace revision, developer d2fad59 (plus room-janitor core/index/tests): static review PASS
- end_room keeps the 42501 host gate and only adds grace_until = now()+30m. A second end_room is denied because is_room_host requires deleted_at null, so grace can't be reset. The account-deletion end (0021) sets only deleted_at, so it gets no grace. is_grace_member requires deleted_at not null and grace_until > now().

The cron keeps the same job name and command at */5. The janitor re-checks isRoomDue in code before it deletes.
- N3 (non-blocking): the two chained .or() calls rely on PostgREST ANDing repeated `or` params. The code re-check keeps this safe, but there is a starvation risk if PostgREST does not AND them. Fix: one and=(...) filter, or confirm in the smoke step. N4: grace can run up to 30 minutes past expires_at (follows the human decision).
- NOT RUN against a DB. Sent to developer: the rollback test, then apply, then the room-janitor deploy may proceed under the human's 2026-11-12 authorization. The qrewing deploy waits for the 00dd3b7 review.
